So it'd sure would be worthwhile to repair before putting it for sale.","type":"example"},{"text":"Cleanliness is a virtue worth more than others.","type":"example"},{"text":"A painting once thought to be worth thousands that is actually not worth much.","type":"example"},{"text":"Is it well worth visiting your hometown? -Nah, Leicester is not worth it at all.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["Having a value of; proper to be exchanged for."],"tags":["not-comparable"]},{"examples":[{"text":"This rickety beater of a car isn’t worth repairing anymore.","type":"example"},{"text":"He found going to the Edinburgh Castle was worth it.","type":"example"},{"text":"I think you’ll find my proposal worth your attention.","type":"example"},{"text":"I didn't think it worth complaining about his friendship not being worth having.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["Deserving of."],"tags":["not-comparable"]},{"glosses":["Valuable, worthwhile."],"qualifier":"except in Scots","tags":["not-comparable","obsolete"]},{"examples":[{"text":"This job is hardly worth the effort.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["Making a fair equivalent of, repaying or compensating."],"tags":["not-comparable"]}],"sounds":[{"ipa":"/ˈwɜːθ/","tags":["Australia","Received-Pronunciation"]},{"ipa":"/wɝθ/","tags":["General-American"]},{"ipa":"/ˈwəːθ/","tags":["British","Southern","Standard"]},{"ipa":"/ˈwøːθ/","tags":["New-Zealand","Wales"]},{"ipa":"/ˈwʌɾθ/","tags":["Scotland"]},{"ipa":"/ˈweːθ/"},{"ipa":"/ˈwɛːθ/"}],"upstream_line":240732,"upstream_offset":1194601739,"upstream_record_sha256":"ecada3a8cc2de3682af5cca2b654eb3b56fe3231092d5745cba276e4ba0edd76","word":"worth"}
